```python
def test_verwaltung_erneut_einladen(app, admin):
    """Review Focus 2: nach Ablehnen und nach Zurueckziehen entsteht eine neue Einladung."""
    from app import db
    L, M = _mk(app, admin, "verneut", "verneutm")
    tid = _new_team(L, "Nochmal")
    first = L.post(f"/api/teams/{tid}/invites", json={"username": M.username}, headers=H).json()["id"]
    assert M.post(f"/api/team-invites/{first}/decline", headers=H).json() == {"ok": True}
    assert db.q1("SELECT status FROM team_invites WHERE id=?", (first,))["status"] == "abgelehnt"
    second = L.post(f"/api/teams/{tid}/invites", json={"username": M.username}, headers=H)
    assert second.status_code == 200 and second.json()["id"] != first
    assert L.delete(f"/api/teams/{tid}/invites/{second.json()['id']}", headers=H).json() == {"ok": True}
    third = L.post(f"/api/teams/{tid}/invites", json={"username": M.username}, headers=H)
    assert third.status_code == 200
    assert [i["id"] for i in M.get("/api/teams").json()["invites"]] == [third.json()["id"]]
    assert [a["action"] for a in db.q("SELECT action FROM audit WHERE action LIKE 'team_invite_%' AND detail LIKE ? "
                                      "ORDER BY id", (f"Team {tid} %",))] == ["team_invite_declined", "team_invite_revoked"]


def test_verwaltung_zurueckziehen_und_fremde_einladung(app, admin):
    L, M, F = _mk(app, admin, "vzur", "vzurm", "vzuro")
    tid = _new_team(L, "Zurueck")
    iid = L.post(f"/api/teams/{tid}/invites", json={"username": M.username}, headers=H).json()["id"]
    assert F.post(f"/api/team-invites/{iid}/accept", headers=H).status_code == 404       # fremde Einladung
    assert F.post(f"/api/team-invites/{iid}/decline", headers=H).status_code == 404
    assert L.delete(f"/api/teams/{tid}/invites/{iid}", headers=H).status_code == 200
    r = L.delete(f"/api/teams/{tid}/invites/{iid}", headers=H)
    assert r.status_code == 409 and r.json()["detail"] == "Einladung nicht mehr offen"
    assert M.post(f"/api/team-invites/{iid}/accept", headers=H).status_code == 409
    assert M.get("/api/teams").json()["invites"] == []
    assert _last_audit("team_invite_revoked")["detail"] == f"Team {tid} „Zurueck“: Einladung an {M.username} zurückgezogen"


def test_verwaltung_einladen_grenze(app, admin):
    """Hoechstens 20 Versuche je Stunde ueber alle Teams; abgewiesene Versuche zaehlen mit."""
    from app import db
    (L,) = _mk(app, admin, "vgrenz")
    t1, t2 = _new_team(L, "A"), _new_team(L, "B")
    for i in range(10):
        assert L.post(f"/api/teams/{t1}/invites", json={"username": f"niemand{i}"}, headers=H).status_code == 404
        assert L.post(f"/api/teams/{t2}/invites", json={"username": f"keiner{i}"}, headers=H).status_code == 404
    r = L.post(f"/api/teams/{t1}/invites", json={"username": "niemand99"}, headers=H)
    assert r.status_code == 429 and "20 je Stunde" in r.json()["detail"]
    assert db.q1("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM audit WHERE user_id=? AND action='team_invite_failed'", (L.uid,))["n"] == 20


def test_verwaltung_rollen_letzter_leiter(app, admin):
    L, M = _mk(app, admin, "vrolle", "vrollem")
    tid = _new_team(L, "Rollen")
    _join(L, M, tid)
    r = L.patch(f"/api/teams/{tid}/members/{L.uid}", json={"role": "mitglied"}, headers=H)
    assert r.status_code == 409 and r.json()["detail"] == "Erst einen anderen Leiter bestimmen"
    assert L.patch(f"/api/teams/{tid}/members/{M.uid}", json={"role": "leiter"}, headers=H).status_code == 200
    r = L.patch(f"/api/teams/{tid}/members/{L.uid}", json={"role": "mitglied"}, headers=H)
    assert r.status_code == 200 and r.json()["my_role"] == "mitglied"
    assert L.patch(f"/api/teams/{tid}/members/{M.uid}", json={"role": "lesend"}, headers=H).status_code == 403
    assert M.patch(f"/api/teams/{tid}/members/{L.uid}", json={"role": "chef"}, headers=H).status_code == 400
    assert M.patch(f"/api/teams/{tid}/members/999999", json={"role": "lesend"}, headers=H).status_code == 404
    assert _last_audit("team_role")["detail"] == f"Team {tid} „Rollen“: {L.username} → Mitglied"


def test_verwaltung_entfernen_und_austreten(app, admin):
    L, M, R = _mk(app, admin, "vent", "ventm", "ventr")
    tid = _new_team(L, "Raus")
    _join(L, M, tid)
    _join(L, R, tid, "lesend")
    assert M.delete(f"/api/teams/{tid}/members/{R.uid}", headers=H).status_code == 403
    assert L.delete(f"/api/teams/{tid}/members/{R.uid}", headers=H).json() == {"ok": True, "team_deleted": False}
    assert R.get(f"/api/teams/{tid}").status_code == 404
    assert _last_audit("team_member_removed")["detail"] == f"Team {tid} „Raus“: {R.username} entfernt"
    assert M.delete(f"/api/teams/{tid}/members/{M.uid}", headers=H).json() == {"ok": True, "team_deleted": False}
    assert _last_audit("team_left")["detail"] == f"Team {tid} „Raus“: {M.username} ausgetreten"
    assert L.delete(f"/api/teams/{tid}/members/999999", headers=H).status_code == 404


def test_verwaltung_letzter_leiter_austritt(app, admin):
    from app import db
    L, M = _mk(app, admin, "vletzt", "vletztm")
    tid = _new_team(L, "Letzter")
    _join(L, M, tid)
    r = L.delete(f"/api/teams/{tid}/members/{L.uid}", headers=H)
    assert r.status_code == 409 and r.json()["detail"] == "Erst einen anderen Leiter bestimmen"
    assert M.delete(f"/api/teams/{tid}/members/{M.uid}", headers=H).status_code == 200
    pid = _project(L.uid, tid)
    r = L.delete(f"/api/teams/{tid}/members/{L.uid}", headers=H)
    assert r.status_code == 409 and r.json()["detail"].startswith("Das Team hat noch 1 Projekt")
    db.ex("DELETE FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,))
    assert L.delete(f"/api/teams/{tid}/members/{L.uid}", headers=H).json() == {"ok": True, "team_deleted": True}
    assert db.q1("SELECT id FROM teams WHERE id=?", (tid,)) is None
    assert _last_audit("team_deleted")["detail"].startswith(f"Team {tid} „Letzter“ gelöscht")


def test_verwaltung_loeschen_leer_und_nicht_leer(app, admin):
    from app import db
    L, M = _mk(app, admin, "vloe", "vloem")
    tid = _new_team(L, "Loeschen")
    _join(L, M, tid)
    assert M.delete(f"/api/teams/{tid}", headers=H).status_code == 403
    p1 = _project(L.uid, tid)
    r = L.delete(f"/api/teams/{tid}", headers=H)
    assert r.status_code == 409 and r.json()["detail"] == "Das Team hat noch 1 Projekt"
    p2 = _project(M.uid, tid)
    assert L.delete(f"/api/teams/{tid}", headers=H).json()["detail"] == "Das Team hat noch 2 Projekte"
    db.ex("DELETE FROM projects WHERE id IN (?,?)", (p1, p2))
    assert L.delete(f"/api/teams/{tid}", headers=H).json() == {"ok": True}
    assert db.q1("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM team_members WHERE team_id=?", (tid,))["n"] == 0
    assert L.get("/api/teams").json()["teams"] == []
    assert _last_audit("team_deleted")["detail"] == f"Team {tid} „Loeschen“ gelöscht (0 Projekte)"
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `web/app/teams.py` anlegen**

```python
"""Teams mit gemeinsamen Bibliotheken: Verwaltung, Einladungen, Rollen und Austritt (Spec Teams §3)."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from . import access, auth, db

router = APIRouter()
ROLES = ("leiter", "mitglied", "lesend")
ROLE_TEXT = {"leiter": "Leiter", "mitglied": "Mitglied", "lesend": "Lesend"}
INVITES_PER_HOUR = 20


def plural(n: int, one: str, many: str) -> str:
    return f"{n} {one if n == 1 else many}"


def team_text(t: dict) -> str:
    return f"Team {t['id']} „{t['name']}“"


def _audit(action: str, detail: str, u: dict, request: Request | None = None):
    db.audit(action, detail, user_id=u["id"], actor=u["username"], ip=auth.client_ip(request) if request else None)


def _clean_name(raw: str) -> str:
    name = (raw or "").strip()
    if not 1 <= len(name) <= 80:
        raise HTTPException(400, "Der Teamname muss 1 bis 80 Zeichen lang sein.")
    return name


def _role(role: str) -> str:
    if role not in ROLES:
        raise HTTPException(400, "Unbekannte Rolle")
    return role


def n_projects(tid: int) -> int:
    return db.q1("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM projects WHERE team_id=?", (tid,))["n"]


def _leaders(tid: int) -> int:
    return db.q1("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM team_members WHERE team_id=? AND role='leiter'", (tid,))["n"]


def _members(tid: int) -> list[dict]:
    return db.q("""SELECT m.user_id, u.username, u.display_name, m.role, m.joined_at FROM team_members m
                   JOIN users u ON u.id=m.user_id WHERE m.team_id=? ORDER BY m.joined_at, m.user_id""", (tid,))


def _open_invites(tid: int) -> list[dict]:
    return db.q("""SELECT i.id, i.user_id, u.username, i.role, b.username invited_by_name, i.created_at
                   FROM team_invites i JOIN users u ON u.id=i.user_id LEFT JOIN users b ON b.id=i.invited_by
                   WHERE i.team_id=? AND i.status='offen' ORDER BY i.id""", (tid,))


def detail(t: dict, my_role: str | None, with_invites: bool) -> dict:
    """Team mit Mitgliedern; offene Einladungen nur fuer Leiter und Admin. Keine Projektinhalte (Spec §5, §8)."""
    return {"id": t["id"], "name": t["name"], "created_at": t["created_at"], "my_role": my_role,
            "n_projects": n_projects(t["id"]), "members": _members(t["id"]),
            "invites": _open_invites(t["id"]) if with_invites else []}


def _member(tid: int, uid: int) -> dict:
    m = db.q1("""SELECT m.*, u.username FROM team_members m JOIN users u ON u.id=m.user_id
                 WHERE m.team_id=? AND m.user_id=?""", (tid, uid))
    if not m:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Mitglied nicht gefunden")
    return m


def _revoke(t: dict, iid: int, u: dict, request: Request):
    with db.atomic():
        inv = db.q1("SELECT i.*, x.username FROM team_invites i JOIN users x ON x.id=i.user_id WHERE i.id=? AND i.team_id=?",
                    (iid, t["id"]))
        if not inv:
            raise HTTPException(404, "Einladung nicht gefunden")
        if inv["status"] != "offen":
            raise HTTPException(409, "Einladung nicht mehr offen")
        db.ex("UPDATE team_invites SET status='zurueckgezogen', decided_at=? WHERE id=?", (db.now(), iid))
        _audit("team_invite_revoked", f"{team_text(t)}: Einladung an {inv['username']} zurückgezogen", u, request)
    return {"ok": True}


def _my_invite(iid: int, u: dict) -> dict:
    """Nur der Eingeladene; fremde Einladungen sind nicht erkennbar (404)."""
    inv = db.q1("SELECT * FROM team_invites WHERE id=? AND user_id=?", (iid, u["id"]))
    if not inv:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Einladung nicht gefunden")
    if inv["status"] != "offen":
        raise HTTPException(409, "Einladung nicht mehr offen")
    return inv


class TeamIn(BaseModel):
    name: str = Field(max_length=200)


class TeamInviteIn(BaseModel):
    username: str = Field(max_length=64)
    role: str = "mitglied"


class RoleIn(BaseModel):
    role: str = Field(max_length=20)


@router.get("/api/teams")
def teams_list(u: dict = Depends(auth.require_user)):
    """Meine Teams mit Rolle, Mitglieder- und Projektzahl; dazu meine offenen Einladungen."""
    rows = db.q("""SELECT t.id, t.name, t.created_at, m.role my_role,
                   (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM team_members x WHERE x.team_id=t.id) n_members,
                   (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM projects p WHERE p.team_id=t.id) n_projects
                   FROM teams t JOIN team_members m ON m.team_id=t.id AND m.user_id=?
                   ORDER BY t.name COLLATE NOCASE, t.id""", (u["id"],))
    invites = db.q("""SELECT i.id, i.team_id, t.name team_name, i.role, b.username invited_by_name, i.created_at
                      FROM team_invites i JOIN teams t ON t.id=i.team_id LEFT JOIN users b ON b.id=i.invited_by
                      WHERE i.user_id=? AND i.status='offen' ORDER BY i.id""", (u["id"],))
    return {"teams": rows, "invites": invites}


@router.post("/api/teams")
def team_create(body: TeamIn, request: Request, u: dict = Depends(auth.require_user)):
    name = _clean_name(body.name)
    with db.atomic():
        tid = db.ex("INSERT INTO teams(name,created_by,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?)", (name, u["id"], db.now()))
        db.ex("INSERT INTO team_members(team_id,user_id,role,joined_at) VALUES (?,?,'leiter',?)", (tid, u["id"], db.now()))
        t = db.q1("SELECT * FROM teams WHERE id=?", (tid,))
        _audit("team_created", f"{team_text(t)} gegründet", u, request)
    return detail(t, "leiter", True)


@router.get("/api/teams/{tid}")
def team_get(tid: int, u: dict = Depends(auth.require_user)):
    t = access.require_team(u, tid, "lesend")
    return detail(t, t["my_role"], t["my_role"] == "leiter")


@router.patch("/api/teams/{tid}")
def team_rename(tid: int, body: TeamIn, request: Request, u: dict = Depends(auth.require_user)):
    t = access.require_team(u, tid, "leiter")
    name = _clean_name(body.name)
    if name != t["name"]:
        db.ex("UPDATE teams SET name=? WHERE id=?", (name, tid))
        _audit("team_renamed", f"Team {tid}: „{t['name']}“ → „{name}“", u, request)
    t = access.require_team(u, tid, "leiter")
    return detail(t, t["my_role"], True)


@router.delete("/api/teams/{tid}")
def team_delete(tid: int, request: Request, u: dict = Depends(auth.require_user)):
    """Nur ein leeres Team (Spec §3); mit Inhalt loescht nur der Admin (teams.admin_team_delete)."""
    with db.atomic():
        t = access.require_team(u, tid, "leiter")
        n = n_projects(tid)
        if n:
            raise HTTPException(409, f"Das Team hat noch {plural(n, 'Projekt', 'Projekte')}")
        db.ex("DELETE FROM teams WHERE id=?", (tid,))
        _audit("team_deleted", f"{team_text(t)} gelöscht (0 Projekte)", u, request)
    return {"ok": True}


@router.post("/api/teams/{tid}/invites")
def team_invite(tid: int, body: TeamInviteIn, request: Request, u: dict = Depends(auth.require_user)):
    """Einladung ueber den exakten Benutzernamen (Gross/klein egal). Hoechstens 20 Versuche je Benutzer und Stunde,
    gezaehlt ueber team_invite und team_invite_failed – auch abgewiesene, damit Namen nicht durchprobiert werden."""
    t = access.require_team(u, tid, "leiter")
    role = _role(body.role)
    name = body.username.strip()
    with db._lock:   # Zaehlen, Pruefen und Eintragen ohne Wettlauf; ein abgewiesener Versuch bleibt protokolliert
        since = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=1)).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
        n = db.q1("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM audit WHERE user_id=? AND action IN ('team_invite','team_invite_failed') "
                  "AND ts>=?", (u["id"], since))["n"]
        if n >= INVITES_PER_HOUR:
            raise HTTPException(429, f"Zu viele Einladungen – höchstens {INVITES_PER_HOUR} je Stunde.")
        target = db.q1("SELECT * FROM users WHERE username=?", (name,)) if name else None   # Spalte COLLATE NOCASE
        problem = None
        if not target or target["status"] != "active":
            problem = (404, "Benutzer nicht gefunden", "unbekannt" if not target else "deaktiviert")
        elif target["id"] == u["id"]:
            problem = (400, "Du kannst dich nicht selbst einladen.", "sich selbst")
        elif access.team_role(target["id"], tid):
            problem = (409, "Ist bereits Mitglied dieses Teams.", "schon Mitglied")
        elif db.q1("SELECT id FROM team_invites WHERE team_id=? AND user_id=? AND status='offen'", (tid, target["id"])):
            problem = (409, "Es gibt schon eine offene Einladung für diesen Benutzer.", "doppelt")
        if problem:
            _audit("team_invite_failed", f"{team_text(t)}: {name[:64]} – {problem[2]}", u, request)
            raise HTTPException(problem[0], problem[1])
        with db.atomic():
            iid = db.ex("INSERT INTO team_invites(team_id,user_id,role,invited_by,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)",
                        (tid, target["id"], role, u["id"], db.now()))
            _audit("team_invite", f"{team_text(t)}: {target['username']} als {ROLE_TEXT[role]} eingeladen", u, request)
    return {"id": iid, "user_id": target["id"], "username": target["username"], "role": role, "status": "offen"}


@router.delete("/api/teams/{tid}/invites/{iid}")
def team_invite_revoke(tid: int, iid: int, request: Request, u: dict = Depends(auth.require_user)):
    return _revoke(access.require_team(u, tid, "leiter"), iid, u, request)


@router.post("/api/team-invites/{iid}/accept")
def team_invite_accept(iid: int, request: Request, u: dict = Depends(auth.require_user)):
    with db.atomic():   # nur bei Status offen, in einer Transaktion (Spec §3)
        inv = _my_invite(iid, u)
        t = db.q1("SELECT * FROM teams WHERE id=?", (inv["team_id"],))
        db.ex("UPDATE team_invites SET status='angenommen', decided_at=? WHERE id=?", (db.now(), iid))
        if not access.team_role(u["id"], t["id"]):
            db.ex("INSERT INTO team_members(team_id,user_id,role,joined_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?)",
                  (t["id"], u["id"], inv["role"], db.now()))
        _audit("team_invite_accepted", f"{team_text(t)}: {u['username']} ist beigetreten ({ROLE_TEXT[inv['role']]})",
               u, request)
    t = access.require_team(u, inv["team_id"], "lesend")
    return detail(t, t["my_role"], t["my_role"] == "leiter")


@router.post("/api/team-invites/{iid}/decline")
def team_invite_decline(iid: int, request: Request, u: dict = Depends(auth.require_user)):
    with db.atomic():
        inv = _my_invite(iid, u)
        t = db.q1("SELECT * FROM teams WHERE id=?", (inv["team_id"],))
        db.ex("UPDATE team_invites SET status='abgelehnt', decided_at=? WHERE id=?", (db.now(), iid))
        _audit("team_invite_declined", f"{team_text(t)}: {u['username']} hat abgelehnt", u, request)
    return {"ok": True}


@router.patch("/api/teams/{tid}/members/{uid}")
def team_member_role(tid: int, uid: int, body: RoleIn, request: Request, u: dict = Depends(auth.require_user)):
    role = _role(body.role)
    with db.atomic():
        t = access.require_team(u, tid, "leiter")
        m = _member(tid, uid)
        if m["role"] == "leiter" and role != "leiter" and _leaders(tid) <= 1:
            raise HTTPException(409, "Erst einen anderen Leiter bestimmen")
        if role != m["role"]:
            db.ex("UPDATE team_members SET role=? WHERE team_id=? AND user_id=?", (role, tid, uid))
            _audit("team_role", f"{team_text(t)}: {m['username']} → {ROLE_TEXT[role]}", u, request)
    t = access.require_team(u, tid, "lesend")   # hat sich der Leiter selbst herabgestuft, gilt die neue Rolle
    return detail(t, t["my_role"], t["my_role"] == "leiter")


@router.delete("/api/teams/{tid}/members/{uid}")
def team_member_remove(tid: int, uid: int, request: Request, u: dict = Depends(auth.require_user)):
    """Leiter entfernen andere; jeder kann austreten. Team-Projekte bleiben im Team (Spec §3)."""
    with db.atomic():
        t = access.require_team(u, tid, "lesend")
        if uid != u["id"] and t["my_role"] != "leiter":
            raise HTTPException(403, access.DENY_LEADER)
        m = _member(tid, uid)
        others = db.q1("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM team_members WHERE team_id=? AND user_id<>?", (tid, uid))["n"]
        if others == 0:      # letztes Mitglied: nur ein leeres Team, das dabei geloescht wird
            n = n_projects(tid)
            if n:
                raise HTTPException(409, f"Das Team hat noch {plural(n, 'Projekt', 'Projekte')} – erst löschen oder verschieben")
            db.ex("DELETE FROM teams WHERE id=?", (tid,))
            _audit("team_left", f"{team_text(t)}: {m['username']} ausgetreten", u, request)
            _audit("team_deleted", f"{team_text(t)} gelöscht (letztes Mitglied ausgetreten, 0 Projekte)", u, request)
            return {"ok": True, "team_deleted": True}
        if m["role"] == "leiter" and _leaders(tid) <= 1:
            raise HTTPException(409, "Erst einen anderen Leiter bestimmen")
        db.ex("DELETE FROM team_members WHERE team_id=? AND user_id=?", (tid, uid))
        if uid == u["id"]:
            _audit("team_left", f"{team_text(t)}: {m['username']} ausgetreten", u, request)
        else:
            _audit("team_member_removed", f"{team_text(t)}: {m['username']} entfernt", u, request)
    return {"ok": True, "team_deleted": False}
```

### Task 5: Projekte – Bereiche, Felder, Anlegen im Team, Verschieben, Löschen (T4)

**Files:**
- Modify: `web/app/access.py` (neue Funktionen `project_fields`, `job_fields` am Dateiende)
- Create: `web/app/projects.py`
- Modify: `web/app/main.py` (Importzeile; `_project_public` Z. 746–763; Route `projects` Z. 788–791 → `project_list`; `ProjectIn` Z. 794–795; `project_new` Z. 798–802; `project_current` Z. 805–812; `project_get` Z. 815–817; `project_patch` letzte Zeile; `project_delete` Z. 889–894; neue Route `project_move` nach `project_delete`; `job_get`)
- Test: `tests/test_teams.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `access.require_project/require_team/project_role/team_role/job_role` (Task 2), `teams`-Tabellen (Task 1).
- Produces:
  - `access.project_fields(user: dict, project: dict) -> dict` – `{team_id, team_name, my_role, creator: {id, name} | None, creator_is_member: bool | None}` (`creator_is_member` nur bei Team-Projekten, sonst `None`; `name` = Anzeigename, sonst Benutzername).
  - `access.job_fields(user: dict, job: dict) -> dict` – `{my_role, team_id, team_name}`.
  - `projects.delete_projects(pids: list[int], then: Callable[[], None] | None = None) -> None` – (1) Aufträge ohne Ersteller dieser Projekte per `jobs.delete` außerhalb von `db.atomic`, (2) in `db.atomic`: Foto-ids sammeln, Projektzeilen löschen, `then()`, (3) nach dem COMMIT Fotoordner löschen.
  - `main._project_public(p: dict, u: dict, detail: bool = False) -> dict` (neuer Parameter `u`), Route `GET /api/projects?scope=private|team:<id>|all` (Funktion `project_list`), `POST /api/projects {name, team_id?}`, `POST /api/projects/{pid}/move {team_id | null}` → Projekt (Detail), `GET /api/jobs/{jid}` mit `my_role`, `team_id`, `team_name`.
  - Konstanten `main.MOVE_FOREIGN`, `main.SAME_AREA`, `main.TEAM_TO_TEAM` (auch für Task 12).

```python
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ T4: Bereiche, Anlegen, Verschieben

def _ids(c, scope=None):
    r = c.get("/api/projects" + (f"?scope={scope}" if scope is not None else ""))
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    return [p["id"] for p in r.json()]


def test_rechte_bereiche_liste(app, admin):
    from app import db
    L, M, F = _mk(app, admin, "bleiter", "bmitgl", "baussen")
    tid = _team("Bereich", {L.uid: "leiter", M.uid: "mitglied"})
    priv = L.post("/api/projects", json={"name": "Privat"}, headers=H).json()
    tp = L.post("/api/projects", json={"name": "Team", "team_id": tid}, headers=H).json()
    assert (tp["team_id"], tp["team_name"], tp["my_role"], tp["creator_is_member"]) == (tid, "Bereich", "leiter", True)
    assert tp["creator"] == {"id": L.uid, "name": L.display}
    assert (priv["team_id"], priv["team_name"], priv["my_role"], priv["creator_is_member"]) == (None, None, "besitzer", None)
    assert _ids(L) == _ids(L, "private") == [priv["id"]]
    assert _ids(L, f"team:{tid}") == [tp["id"]]
    assert set(_ids(L, "all")) == {priv["id"], tp["id"]}
    assert _ids(M, "all") == [tp["id"]] and _ids(M, "private") == []
    entry = M.get(f"/api/projects?scope=team:{tid}").json()[0]
    assert entry["my_role"] == "mitglied" and {"thumb", "last_job", "updated_at"} <= set(entry)
    assert F.get(f"/api/projects?scope=team:{tid}").status_code == 404
    assert L.get("/api/projects?scope=quatsch").status_code == 400
    assert L.get("/api/projects?scope=team:²").status_code == 400
    mp = M.post("/api/projects", json={"name": "von M", "team_id": tid}, headers=H).json()["id"]
    db.ex("DELETE FROM team_members WHERE team_id=? AND user_id=?", (tid, M.uid))       # M ist ehemaliges Mitglied
    e = next(p for p in L.get(f"/api/projects?scope=team:{tid}").json() if p["id"] == mp)
    assert e["creator"] == {"id": M.uid, "name": M.display} and e["creator_is_member"] is False
    db.ex("UPDATE projects SET user_id=NULL WHERE id=?", (mp,))                          # geloeschter Ersteller
    e = next(p for p in L.get(f"/api/projects?scope=team:{tid}").json() if p["id"] == mp)
    assert e["creator"] is None and e["creator_is_member"] is False


def test_rechte_anlegen_im_team(app, admin):
    from app import access
    L, M, R, F = _mk(app, admin, "aleiter", "amitgl", "alesend", "aaussen")
    tid = _team("Anlegen", {L.uid: "leiter", M.uid: "mitglied", R.uid: "lesend"})
    assert M.post("/api/projects", json={"name": "x", "team_id": tid}, headers=H).status_code == 200
    r = R.post("/api/projects", json={"name": "x", "team_id": tid}, headers=H)
    assert r.status_code == 403 and r.json()["detail"] == access.DENY_WRITE
    assert F.post("/api/projects", json={"name": "x", "team_id": tid}, headers=H).status_code == 404
    assert L.post("/api/projects", json={"name": "y", "team_id": 999999}, headers=H).status_code == 404


def test_rechte_current_bleibt_privat(app, admin):

    assert cur["team_id"] is None and cur["id"] != tp and cur["user_id"] == L.uid
    assert L.get("/api/projects/current").json()["id"] == cur["id"]


def test_rechte_nach_austritt_404_und_current(app, admin):
    """Review Focus 4: Mitgliedschaft weg, Seite noch offen -> naechste Anfrage 404, current liefert privat."""
    L, M = _mk(app, admin, "nleiter", "nmitgl")
    tid = _team("Austritt", {L.uid: "leiter", M.uid: "mitglied"})
    tp = M.post("/api/projects", json={"name": "Meins", "team_id": tid}, headers=H).json()["id"]
    jid = _job(M.uid, tp)
    assert M.get(f"/api/projects/{tp}").status_code == 200
    assert L.delete(f"/api/teams/{tid}/members/{M.uid}", headers=H).status_code == 200
    r = M.get(f"/api/projects/{tp}")
    assert r.status_code == 404 and r.json()["detail"] == "Projekt nicht gefunden"
    assert M.get(f"/api/jobs/{jid}").status_code == 404
    assert M.get("/api/projects/current").json()["team_id"] is None
    assert L.get(f"/api/projects/{tp}").json()["creator_is_member"] is False        # Projekt bleibt im Team


def test_rechte_auftrag_felder(app, admin):
    L, R = _mk(app, admin, "fleiter", "flesend")

    assert (j["my_role"], j["team_id"], j["team_name"]) == ("lesend", tid, "Felder")
    assert L.get(f"/api/jobs/{_job(L.uid, _project(L.uid))}").json()["my_role"] == "besitzer"
    j3 = L.get(f"/api/jobs/{_job(L.uid, None)}").json()
    assert (j3["my_role"], j3["team_id"], j3["team_name"]) == ("besitzer", None, None)


def test_verschieben_privat_ins_team_mit_auftraegen(app, admin):
    from app import db
    L, M = _mk(app, admin, "vsleiter", "vsmitgl")
    tid = _team("Ziel", {L.uid: "leiter", M.uid: "mitglied"})
    pid = L.post("/api/projects", json={"name": "Wandert"}, headers=H).json()["id"]
    jid = _job(L.uid, pid)
    assert M.get(f"/api/jobs/{jid}").status_code == 404
    r = L.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/move", json={"team_id": tid}, headers=H)
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    assert (r.json()["team_id"], r.json()["user_id"], r.json()["team_name"]) == (tid, L.uid, "Ziel")
    assert M.get(f"/api/projects/{pid}").status_code == 200 and M.get(f"/api/jobs/{jid}").status_code == 200
    assert db.q1("SELECT project_id FROM jobs WHERE id=?", (jid,))["project_id"] == pid      # Auftrag folgt dem Projekt
    a = _last_audit("project_move")
    assert a["detail"] == f"Projekt {pid}: privat → Team {tid} „Ziel“" and a["actor"] == L.username


def test_verschieben_team_nach_privat(app, admin):

    r = L.post(f"/api/projects/{pid}/move", json={"team_id": None}, headers=H)
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    assert (r.json()["team_id"], r.json()["user_id"], r.json()["my_role"]) == (None, L.uid, "besitzer")
    assert M.get(f"/api/projects/{pid}").status_code == 404
    assert _last_audit("project_move")["detail"] == f"Projekt {pid}: Team {tid} „Zurueck“ → privat"

def test_verschieben_rechte_und_ziele(app, admin):
    from app import access
    L, M, R, F = _mk(app, admin, "vrleiter", "vrmitgl", "vrlesend", "vraussen")
    tid = _team("Start", {L.uid: "leiter", M.uid: "mitglied", R.uid: "lesend"})
    t_o = _team("Fremdes", {F.uid: "leiter"})
    t_ro = _team("Nur lesen", {L.uid: "lesend"})
    t_l = _team("Zweites", {L.uid: "leiter"})
    tp_l, tp_m, priv = _project(L.uid, tid), _project(M.uid, tid), _project(L.uid)
    assert M.post(f"/api/projects/{tp_l}/move", json={"team_id": None}, headers=H).status_code == 403   # fremd angelegt
    r = R.post(f"/api/projects/{tp_m}/move", json={"team_id": None}, headers=H)
    assert r.status_code == 403 and r.json()["detail"] == access.DENY_WRITE
    assert F.post(f"/api/projects/{tp_l}/move", json={"team_id": None}, headers=H).status_code == 404
    assert L.post(f"/api/projects/{priv}/move", json={"team_id": t_o}, headers=H).status_code == 404   # kein Mitglied
    r = L.post(f"/api/projects/{priv}/move", json={"team_id": t_ro}, headers=H)
    assert r.status_code == 403 and r.json()["detail"] == access.DENY_WRITE                           # Ziel nur lesend
    r = L.post(f"/api/projects/{priv}/move", json={"team_id": None}, headers=H)
    assert r.status_code == 400 and r.json()["detail"] == "Das Projekt liegt bereits in diesem Bereich."
    assert L.post(f"/api/projects/{tp_l}/move", json={"team_id": tid}, headers=H).status_code == 400
    r = L.post(f"/api/projects/{tp_l}/move", json={"team_id": t_l}, headers=H)
    assert r.status_code == 400 and "In privat kopieren" in r.json()["detail"]                         # Team -> Team
    r = admin.post(f"/api/projects/{_project(F.uid)}/move", json={"team_id": t_o}, headers=H)
    assert r.status_code == 403 and r.json()["detail"] == access.DENY_OWNER                            # Admin: nie move


def test_benutzer_teamprojekt_loeschen_auftraege_ohne_ersteller(app, admin):
    """Spec §4: Aufträge bleiben beim Ersteller ohne Projekt; Aufträge ohne Ersteller werden mitgelöscht."""
    from app import config, db
    L, M = _mk(app, admin, "lleiter", "lmitgl")
    tid = _team("Loeschen", {L.uid: "leiter", M.uid: "mitglied"})
    pid = _project(M.uid, tid)
    phid = _photo(pid)
    j_l, j_x = _job(L.uid, pid), _job(M.uid, pid)
    db.ex("UPDATE jobs SET user_id=NULL WHERE id=?", (j_x,))
    assert L.delete(f"/api/projects/{pid}", headers=H).json() == {"ok": True}
    assert db.q1("SELECT project_id FROM jobs WHERE id=?", (j_l,))["project_id"] is None
    assert L.get(f"/api/jobs/{j_l}").status_code == 200
    assert db.q1("SELECT id FROM jobs WHERE id=?", (j_x,)) is None
    assert not (config.DATA_DIR / "jobs" / str(j_x)).exists()
    assert not (config.DATA_DIR / "photos" / str(phid)).exists()
```

```python
def project_fields(user: dict, project: dict) -> dict:
    """Spec Teams §4: team_id, team_name, my_role, creator ({id, name} oder None = geloeschter Benutzer) und
    creator_is_member (nur bei Team-Projekten, sonst None)."""
    out = {"team_id": project.get("team_id"), "team_name": None, "my_role": project_role(user, project),
           "creator": None, "creator_is_member": None}
    if project.get("user_id") is not None:
        c = db.q1("SELECT id, username, display_name FROM users WHERE id=?", (project["user_id"],))
        if c:
            out["creator"] = {"id": c["id"], "name": c["display_name"] or c["username"]}
    if project.get("team_id") is not None:
        t = db.q1("SELECT name FROM teams WHERE id=?", (project["team_id"],))
        out["team_name"] = t["name"] if t else None
        out["creator_is_member"] = team_role(project.get("user_id"), project["team_id"]) is not None
    return out


def job_fields(user: dict, job: dict) -> dict:
    """GET /api/jobs/{jid}: Rolle und Team des Projekts (Lesend-Modus der Auftragsseite)."""
    p = db.q1("SELECT team_id FROM projects WHERE id=?", (job["project_id"],)) if job.get("project_id") else None
    tid = p["team_id"] if p else None
    t = db.q1("SELECT name FROM teams WHERE id=?", (tid,)) if tid is not None else None
    return {"my_role": job_role(user, job), "team_id": tid, "team_name": t["name"] if t else None}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `web/app/projects.py` anlegen**

```python
"""Projekte samt Dateien loeschen (Spec Teams §4, §5): Projekt-Loeschen, Team-Loeschen durch den Admin, Benutzer-Loeschen."""
from __future__ import annotations

import shutil
from collections.abc import Callable

from . import config, db, jobs


def delete_projects(pids: list[int], then: Callable[[], None] | None = None) -> None:
    """(1) Auftraege ohne Ersteller dieser Projekte per jobs.delete – ausserhalb von db.atomic, weil jobs.delete
    jobs._lock nimmt (Sperrreihenfolge jobs._lock -> db._lock); alle anderen Auftraege bleiben bei ihren Erstellern
    ohne Projekt (ON DELETE SET NULL). (2) In einer Transaktion Foto-ids sammeln, Projektzeilen loeschen und then()
    (z. B. Team oder Benutzer loeschen). (3) Nach dem COMMIT die Fotoordner entfernen."""
    marks = ",".join("?" * len(pids))
    if pids:
        for j in db.q(f"SELECT * FROM jobs WHERE project_id IN ({marks}) AND user_id IS NULL", tuple(pids)):
            jobs.delete(j)
    photo_ids: list[int] = []
    with db.atomic():
        if pids:
            photo_ids = [r["id"] for r in db.q(f"SELECT id FROM photos WHERE project_id IN ({marks})", tuple(pids))]
            db.ex(f"DELETE FROM projects WHERE id IN ({marks})", tuple(pids))
        if then is not None:
            then()
    for phid in photo_ids:
        shutil.rmtree(config.DATA_DIR / "photos" / str(phid), ignore_errors=True)
```

```python
def _project_public(p: dict, u: dict, detail: bool = False) -> dict:
    out = dict(p)
    out.update(access.project_fields(u, p))   # team_id, team_name, my_role, creator, creator_is_member (Spec Teams §4)
    out["cad_check"] = check.public_check(p) if detail else None
    out["n_photos"] = db.q1("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM photos WHERE project_id=?", (p["id"],))["n"]
    out["n_measurements"] = db.q1("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM measurements WHERE project_id=? AND accepted=1", (p["id"],))["n"]
    fmt = _project_format(p["id"])
    out["format"] = fmt
    out["factors"] = _factors(p)
    if detail:
        hp = _hints(p)
        out["photos"] = [_photo_public(ph, hp) for ph in db.q("SELECT * FROM photos WHERE project_id=? ORDER BY id", (p["id"],))]
        out["measurements"] = [_meas_public(m) for m in
                               db.q("SELECT * FROM measurements WHERE project_id=? ORDER BY seq", (p["id"],))]
        out["grid_h"] = _grid_h(fmt)
        out["parts"] = parts.public_list(p["id"])
        out["model_dims"] = [{k: r[k] for k in ("id", "label", "grp", "value", "job_id", "dim_id", "created_at")}
                             for r in jobs.learned_dims(p["id"])]
    return out
```

Die Route `projects` (Z. 788–791), `ProjectIn`, `project_new` und `project_current` vollständig ersetzen durch:

```python
@app.get("/api/projects")
def project_list(scope: str = "private", u: dict = Depends(auth.require_user)):
    """Spec Teams §4: private (Standard), team:<id> (nur Mitglieder) oder all (privat und alle eigenen Teams)."""
    if scope == "private":
        rows = db.q("SELECT * FROM projects WHERE user_id=? AND team_id IS NULL ORDER BY archived, id DESC", (u["id"],))
    elif scope == "all":
        rows = db.q("SELECT p.* FROM projects p LEFT JOIN team_members m ON m.team_id=p.team_id AND m.user_id=? "
                    "WHERE (p.team_id IS NULL AND p.user_id=?) OR m.user_id IS NOT NULL ORDER BY p.archived, p.id DESC",
                    (u["id"], u["id"]))
    elif scope.startswith("team:") and scope[5:].isascii() and scope[5:].isdigit():
        tid = int(scope[5:])
        access.require_team(u, tid, "lesend")
        rows = db.q("SELECT * FROM projects WHERE team_id=? ORDER BY archived, id DESC", (tid,))
    else:
        raise HTTPException(400, "Unbekannter Bereich")
    return [{**_project_public(p, u), **_library_fields(p)} for p in rows]


class ProjectIn(BaseModel):
    name: str = Field(default="", max_length=120)
    team_id: int | None = None


@app.post("/api/projects")
def project_new(body: ProjectIn, u: dict = Depends(auth.require_user)):
    name = body.name.strip() or f"Projekt {datetime.now().strftime('%d.%m.%Y %H:%M')}"
    if body.team_id is not None:
        access.require_team(u, body.team_id, "mitglied")    # Spec Teams §4: nur Leiter/Mitglied (sonst 403/404)
    pid = db.ex("INSERT INTO projects(user_id,team_id,name,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?)",
                (u["id"], body.team_id, name, db.now()))
    return _project_public(db.q1("SELECT * FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,)), u, True)


@app.get("/api/projects/current")
def project_current(u: dict = Depends(auth.require_user)):
    """Bleibt privat (Spec Teams §4); legt bei leerem privatem Bereich ein privates Projekt an."""
    p = db.q1("SELECT * FROM projects WHERE user_id=? AND team_id IS NULL AND archived=0 ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1",
              (u["id"],))
    if not p:
        pid = db.ex("INSERT INTO projects(user_id,name,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?)",
                    (u["id"], f"Projekt {datetime.now().strftime('%d.%m.%Y')}", db.now()))
        p = db.q1("SELECT * FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,))
    return _project_public(p, u, True)
```

In `project_get` die Zeile `return _project_public(_own_project(pid, u, "read"), True)` ersetzen durch `return _project_public(_own_project(pid, u, "read"), u, True)`; in `project_patch` die letzte Zeile `return _project_public(p, True)` durch `return _project_public(p, u, True)`.

`project_delete` vollständig ersetzen und direkt dahinter die Verschieben-Route einfügen:

```python
@app.delete("/api/projects/{pid}")
def project_delete(pid: int, u: dict = Depends(auth.require_user)):
    """Spec Teams §4: Fotos, Messungen, Teile, Werte weg; Aufträge bleiben beim Ersteller ohne Projekt, Aufträge
    ohne Ersteller werden mitgelöscht."""
    _own_project(pid, u, "delete")
    projects.delete_projects([pid])
    return {"ok": True}


MOVE_FOREIGN = "Das Projekt enthält Aufträge anderer Mitglieder – bitte „In privat kopieren“ verwenden"
SAME_AREA = "Das Projekt liegt bereits in diesem Bereich."
TEAM_TO_TEAM = "Zwischen zwei Teams geht es nur über „In privat kopieren“ und „In Team kopieren“."


class MoveIn(BaseModel):
    team_id: int | None = None


def _area(team_id: int | None) -> str:
    if team_id is None:
        return "privat"
    t = db.q1("SELECT name FROM teams WHERE id=?", (team_id,))
    return f"Team {team_id} „{t['name'] if t else '?'}“"


@app.post("/api/projects/{pid}/move")
def project_move(pid: int, body: MoveIn, request: Request, u: dict = Depends(auth.require_user)):
    """Spec Teams §4: privat ↔ Team samt Aufträgen. Nach privat gehört das Projekt dem Verschiebenden und geht nur
    ohne Aufträge anderer (auch gelöschter) Ersteller (F-1)."""
    p = _own_project(pid, u, "move")
    if body.team_id == p["team_id"]:
        raise HTTPException(400, SAME_AREA)
    if body.team_id is not None and p["team_id"] is not None:
        raise HTTPException(400, TEAM_TO_TEAM)
    if body.team_id is not None:
        access.require_team(u, body.team_id, "mitglied")
    with db.atomic():
        cur = db.q1("SELECT * FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,))
        if not cur or cur["team_id"] != p["team_id"]:
            raise HTTPException(409, "Das Projekt wurde gerade geändert – bitte neu laden.")
        if body.team_id is None:
            n = db.q1("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM jobs WHERE project_id=? AND (user_id IS NULL OR user_id<>?)",
                      (pid, u["id"]))["n"]
            if n:
                raise HTTPException(409, MOVE_FOREIGN)
            db.ex("UPDATE projects SET team_id=NULL, user_id=? WHERE id=?", (u["id"], pid))
        else:
            db.ex("UPDATE projects SET team_id=? WHERE id=?", (body.team_id, pid))
        db.audit("project_move", f"Projekt {pid}: {_area(p['team_id'])} → {_area(body.team_id)}", user_id=u["id"],
                 actor=u["username"], ip=auth.client_ip(request))
    return _project_public(db.q1("SELECT * FROM projects WHERE id=?", (pid,)), u, True)
```

```python
def job_get(jid: int, u: dict = Depends(auth.require_user)):

    return {**jobs.job_public(j, True), **access.job_fields(u, j)}   # my_role, team_id, team_name (Lesend-Modus)
```

**Files:**

- Modify: `web/app/main.py` (`admin_users` Z. 456–468; `admin_user_delete` Z. 549–568; `_delete_project_files` Z. 884–886 entfernen; `job_list`)
- Test: `tests/test_teams.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `projects.delete_projects` (Task 5), `teams.detail/_member/_revoke/_role/RoleIn/plural/team_text/n_projects` (Task 4), `access.require_team` (Task 2).
- Produces:
  - `teams.succeed_leaders(uid: int, actor: dict) -> None` – in der Transaktion vor `DELETE FROM users`: war `uid` letzter Leiter eines Teams mit weiteren Mitgliedern, wird das älteste verbleibende Mitglied (`joined_at`, dann `user_id`) Leiter; Audit `team_leader_auto`.
  - `GET /api/admin/teams` → `[{id, name, created_at, n_members, n_projects, leaders: [username], no_leader: bool}]`; `GET /api/admin/teams/{tid}` → `teams.detail(t, None, True)`; `PATCH /api/admin/teams/{tid}/members/{uid} {role}` → Detail; `DELETE /api/admin/teams/{tid}/members/{uid}` → Detail; `DELETE /api/admin/teams/{tid}/invites/{iid}` → `{"ok": true}`; `DELETE /api/admin/teams/{tid}?confirm=<Teamname>` → `{"ok": true, "projects": n}` (mit Projekten ohne passenden Namen 409).
  - `GET /api/admin/users` → je Benutzer zusätzlich `team_projects: int` (bleibende Team-Projekte); `jobs` zählt weiter nur selbst angelegte.
  - `DELETE /api/admin/users/{uid}` → `{"ok": true, "team_projects_kept": n}`.
  - `GET /api/jobs?team=<tid>` (Mitglied) und `GET /api/jobs?all=1` (Admin: ohne Projekt, privat, eigene Teams), jeweils mit `username` (`null` = gelöschter Benutzer).

```python
    assert db.q1("SELECT project_id, user_id FROM jobs WHERE id=?", (j_l_in_priv,)) == {"project_id": None, "user_id": L.uid}
    assert db.q1("SELECT user_id, team_id FROM projects WHERE id=?", (tp,)) == {"user_id": None, "team_id": tid}
    assert db.q1("SELECT user_id, project_id FROM jobs WHERE id=?", (j_team,)) == {"user_id": None, "project_id": tp}
    assert db.q1("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM team_members WHERE user_id=?", (M.uid,))["n"] == 0
    assert db.q1("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM team_invites WHERE user_id=?", (M.uid,))["n"] == 0
    e = next(p for p in L.get(f"/api/projects?scope=team:{tid}").json() if p["id"] == tp)
    assert e["creator"] is None and e["creator_is_member"] is False
    assert "1 Team-Projekt bleibt" in _last_audit("user_deleted")["detail"]


def test_benutzer_leiter_nachfolge(app, admin):
    from app import db
    L, A1, A2, B = _mk(app, admin, "blleiter", "blaelt", "bljung", "blzweit")
    erbe = _team("Erbe", {L.uid: "leiter", A1.uid: "mitglied", A2.uid: "lesend"})
    zwei = _team("Zwei Leiter", {L.uid: "leiter", B.uid: "leiter", A1.uid: "mitglied"})
    solo = _team("Allein", {L.uid: "leiter"})
    assert admin.delete(f"/api/admin/users/{L.uid}", headers=H).status_code == 200
    roles = {r["user_id"]: r["role"] for r in db.q("SELECT user_id, role FROM team_members WHERE team_id=?", (erbe,))}
    assert roles == {A1.uid: "leiter", A2.uid: "lesend"}                       # aeltestes verbleibendes Mitglied
    roles2 = {r["user_id"]: r["role"] for r in db.q("SELECT user_id, role FROM team_members WHERE team_id=?", (zwei,))}
    assert roles2 == {B.uid: "leiter", A1.uid: "mitglied"}                     # anderer Leiter da: keine Nachfolge
    autos = db.q("SELECT detail FROM audit WHERE action='team_leader_auto' AND detail LIKE ?",
                 (f"%(Nachfolge für {L.username})",))
    assert [a["detail"] for a in autos] == [f"Team {erbe} „Erbe“: {A1.username} ist jetzt Leiter (Nachfolge für {L.username})"]
    assert db.q1("SELECT id FROM teams WHERE id=?", (solo,))                   # Team ohne Mitglieder bleibt, ohne Leiter
    assert next(t for t in admin.get("/api/admin/teams").json() if t["id"] == solo)["no_leader"] is True


def test_benutzer_auftragsliste_team_filter(app, admin):
    L, M, F = _mk(app, admin, "bfleiter", "bfmitgl", "bfaussen")
    tid = _team("Filter", {L.uid: "leiter", M.uid: "mitglied"})
    tp = _project(L.uid, tid)
    jl, jm = _job(L.uid, tp), _job(M.uid, tp)
    jo = _job(M.uid, None)
    rows = M.get(f"/api/jobs?team={tid}").json()
    assert [(r["id"], r["username"]) for r in rows] == [(jm, M.username), (jl, L.username)]
    assert F.get(f"/api/jobs?team={tid}").status_code == 404
    assert [r["id"] for r in M.get("/api/jobs").json()] == [jo, jm]             # „Meine“ = selbst angelegt
```

Importzeile `from . import access, auth, db` ersetzen durch `from . import access, auth, db, projects`, dann am Dateiende anhängen:

```python
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ Benutzer loeschen: Leiter-Nachfolge (Spec §5)

def succeed_leaders(uid: int, actor: dict):
    """War uid letzter Leiter eines Teams mit weiteren Mitgliedern, wird das aelteste verbleibende Mitglied (fruehestes
    joined_at) Leiter. Laeuft in der Transaktion, die danach die Benutzerzeile loescht."""
    gone = db.q1("SELECT username FROM users WHERE id=?", (uid,))
    for t in db.q("""SELECT t.* FROM teams t JOIN team_members m ON m.team_id=t.id AND m.user_id=? AND m.role='leiter'
                     WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM team_members o WHERE o.team_id=t.id AND o.user_id<>?
                                       AND o.role='leiter')""", (uid, uid)):
        nxt = db.q1("""SELECT m.user_id, u.username FROM team_members m JOIN users u ON u.id=m.user_id
                       WHERE m.team_id=? AND m.user_id<>? ORDER BY m.joined_at, m.user_id LIMIT 1""", (t["id"], uid))
        if nxt:
            db.ex("UPDATE team_members SET role='leiter' WHERE team_id=? AND user_id=?", (t["id"], nxt["user_id"]))
            _audit("team_leader_auto", f"{team_text(t)}: {nxt['username']} ist jetzt Leiter "
                                       f"(Nachfolge für {gone['username'] if gone else uid})", actor)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ Admin-Reiter „Teams“ (Spec §5)

def _team_or_404(tid: int) -> dict:
    t = db.q1("SELECT * FROM teams WHERE id=?", (tid,))
    if not t:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Team nicht gefunden")
    return t


@router.get("/api/admin/teams")
def admin_teams(a: dict = Depends(auth.require_admin)):
    rows = db.q("""SELECT t.id, t.name, t.created_at,
                   (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM team_members x WHERE x.team_id=t.id) n_members,
                   (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM projects p WHERE p.team_id=t.id) n_projects
                   FROM teams t ORDER BY t.name COLLATE NOCASE, t.id""")
    for r in rows:
        r["leaders"] = [x["username"] for x in db.q("SELECT u.username FROM team_members m JOIN users u ON u.id=m.user_id "
                                                     "WHERE m.team_id=? AND m.role='leiter' ORDER BY m.joined_at",
                                                     (r["id"],))]
        r["no_leader"] = not r["leaders"]
    return rows


@router.get("/api/admin/teams/{tid}")
def admin_team_get(tid: int, a: dict = Depends(auth.require_admin)):
    """Mitglieder, Rollen, Einladungen und Projektzahl – keine Inhalte der Team-Projekte."""
    return detail(_team_or_404(tid), None, True)


@router.patch("/api/admin/teams/{tid}/members/{uid}")
def admin_team_role(tid: int, uid: int, body: RoleIn, request: Request, a: dict = Depends(auth.require_admin)):
    """Rolle setzen, auch Leiter ernennen; ohne Pruefung des letzten Leiters (der Admin ist der Ausweg)."""
    role = _role(body.role)
    with db.atomic():
        t = _team_or_404(tid)
        m = _member(tid, uid)
        if role != m["role"]:
            db.ex("UPDATE team_members SET role=? WHERE team_id=? AND user_id=?", (role, tid, uid))
            _audit("team_role", f"{team_text(t)}: {m['username']} → {ROLE_TEXT[role]} (Administrator)", a, request)
    return detail(t, None, True)


@router.delete("/api/admin/teams/{tid}/members/{uid}")
def admin_team_member_remove(tid: int, uid: int, request: Request, a: dict = Depends(auth.require_admin)):
    with db.atomic():
        t = _team_or_404(tid)
        m = _member(tid, uid)
        db.ex("DELETE FROM team_members WHERE team_id=? AND user_id=?", (tid, uid))
        _audit("team_member_removed", f"{team_text(t)}: {m['username']} entfernt (Administrator)", a, request)
    return detail(t, None, True)


@router.delete("/api/admin/teams/{tid}/invites/{iid}")
def admin_team_invite_revoke(tid: int, iid: int, request: Request, a: dict = Depends(auth.require_admin)):
    return _revoke(_team_or_404(tid), iid, a, request)


@router.delete("/api/admin/teams/{tid}")
def admin_team_delete(tid: int, request: Request, confirm: str = "", a: dict = Depends(auth.require_admin)):
    """Team auch mit Projekten loeschen, nach Rueckfrage mit dem Teamnamen. Projekte wie beim Projekt-Loeschen:
    Auftraege ohne Ersteller per jobs.delete (ausserhalb von db.atomic), dann Projektzeilen und Team in einer
    Transaktion, danach die Fotoordner; alle anderen Auftraege bleiben bei ihren Erstellern ohne Projekt."""
    t = _team_or_404(tid)
    pids = [r["id"] for r in db.q("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE team_id=?", (tid,))]
    if pids and confirm.strip() != t["name"]:
        raise HTTPException(409, f"Das Team hat noch {plural(len(pids), 'Projekt', 'Projekte')} – zur Bestätigung "
                                 "den Teamnamen eingeben.")

    def drop_team():
        if db.q1("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM projects WHERE team_id=?", (tid,))["n"]:
            raise HTTPException(409, "Im Team wurde gerade ein Projekt angelegt – bitte erneut löschen.")
        db.ex("DELETE FROM teams WHERE id=?", (tid,))
        _audit("team_deleted", f"{team_text(t)} gelöscht ({plural(len(pids), 'Projekt', 'Projekte')}, Administrator)",
               a, request)
    projects.delete_projects(pids, then=drop_team)
    return {"ok": True, "projects": len(pids)}
```

```python
@app.get("/api/admin/users")
def admin_users(a: dict = Depends(auth.require_admin)):
    users = db.q("SELECT * FROM users ORDER BY username")
    out = []
    for u in users:
        pu = auth.public_user(u)
        pu["usage"] = _usage(u)

        pu["team_projects"] = db.q1("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM projects WHERE user_id=? AND team_id IS NOT NULL",
                                    (u["id"],))["n"]   # bleiben beim Loeschen im Team (Spec Teams §5)
        pu["pending_reset"] = bool(db.q1("SELECT id FROM resets WHERE user_id=? AND used_at IS NULL AND expires_at>?",
                                         (u["id"], db.now())))
        pu["sessions"] = db.q1("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM sessions WHERE user_id=? AND expires_at>?", (u["id"], db.now()))["n"]
```

`admin_user_delete` vollständig ersetzen:

```python
@app.delete("/api/admin/users/{uid}")
def admin_user_delete(uid: int, request: Request, a: dict = Depends(auth.require_admin)):
    """Spec Teams §5: Aufträge ohne Projekt und in privaten Projekten sowie die privaten Projekte samt Dateien weg;
    Team-Projekte und deren Aufträge bleiben (Ersteller leer); Mitgliedschaften und Einladungen per Kaskade;
    Leiter-Nachfolge in derselben Transaktion wie DELETE FROM users."""
    u = db.q1("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id=?", (uid,))
    if not u:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Benutzer nicht gefunden")
    if u["id"] == a["id"]:
        raise HTTPException(400, "Das eigene Konto kann nicht gelöscht werden.")
    with db._lock:
        if u["role"] == "admin" and auth.active_admins() <= 1:
            raise HTTPException(400, "Der letzte Administrator kann nicht gelöscht werden.")
        db.ex("UPDATE users SET status='disabled' WHERE id=?", (uid,))
    auth.end_sessions(uid)
    for j in db.q("SELECT j.* FROM jobs j LEFT JOIN projects p ON p.id=j.project_id WHERE j.user_id=? AND p.team_id IS NULL",
                  (uid,)):
        jobs.delete(j)   # ohne Projekt oder in privaten Projekten; nie in db.atomic()
    private = [r["id"] for r in db.q("SELECT id FROM projects WHERE user_id=? AND team_id IS NULL", (uid,))]
    kept = db.q1("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM projects WHERE user_id=? AND team_id IS NOT NULL", (uid,))["n"]

    def drop_user():
        teams.succeed_leaders(uid, a)
        db.ex("DELETE FROM users WHERE id=?", (uid,))
    projects.delete_projects(private, then=drop_user)
    db.audit("user_deleted", f"„{u['username']}“ mit allen privaten Daten gelöscht, "
                             f"{teams.plural(kept, 'Team-Projekt bleibt', 'Team-Projekte bleiben')}",
             user_id=a["id"], actor=a["username"], ip=auth.client_ip(request))
    return {"ok": True, "team_projects_kept": kept}
```

```python
@app.get("/api/jobs")
def job_list(all: int = 0, team: int | None = None, u: dict = Depends(auth.require_user)):
    """Meine (selbst angelegt), team=<id> (Mitglied: Aufträge der Team-Projekte), all=1 (Admin: ohne Projekt, privat,
    Teams mit Mitgliedschaft). Ersteller per LEFT JOIN users: leer = gelöschter Benutzer (Spec Teams §2, §5)."""
    if team is not None:
        access.require_team(u, team, "lesend")
        rows = db.q("SELECT j.*, us.username FROM jobs j JOIN projects p ON p.id=j.project_id "
                    "LEFT JOIN users us ON us.id=j.user_id WHERE p.team_id=? ORDER BY j.id DESC LIMIT 500", (team,))
    elif all and u["role"] == "admin":
        rows = db.q("SELECT j.*, us.username FROM jobs j LEFT JOIN users us ON us.id=j.user_id "
                    "LEFT JOIN projects p ON p.id=j.project_id WHERE p.id IS NULL OR p.team_id IS NULL "
                    "OR p.team_id IN (SELECT team_id FROM team_members WHERE user_id=?) ORDER BY j.id DESC LIMIT 500",
                    (u["id"],))
    else:
        rows = db.q("SELECT j.*, ? AS username FROM jobs j WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 500",
                    (u["username"], u["id"]))
    return [{**jobs.job_public(r), "username": r["username"]} for r in rows]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `web/static/js/rights.js` anlegen**

```js
// Rechte und Bereiche der Bibliothek ohne DOM (Spec Teams §2, §7) – auch für node --test.
// Nur Komfort für die Oberfläche: der Server prüft jede Aktion selbst (web/app/access.py).

export const ROLE_TEXT = Object.freeze({ besitzer: "Besitzer", admin: "Administrator", leiter: "Leiter", mitglied: "Mitglied", lesend: "Lesend" });

// Was die Oberfläche für ein Projekt anbietet (my_role und creator aus GET /api/projects)
export function projectRights(p, meId) {
  const r = p?.my_role ?? null, own = p?.creator?.id != null && p.creator.id === meId;
  return {
    write: r === "besitzer" || r === "admin" || r === "leiter" || r === "mitglied",
    delete: r === "besitzer" || r === "admin" || r === "leiter" || (r === "mitglied" && own),
    move: r === "besitzer" || r === "leiter" || (r === "mitglied" && own),
    copy: r === "besitzer" || r === "leiter" || r === "mitglied",
  };
}

// Gemerkter Bereich (lib.scope): "private" oder "team:<id>" eines eigenen Teams; alles andere wird "private"
export function validScope(stored, teams) {
  const m = /^team:([1-9][0-9]*)$/.exec(String(stored ?? ""));
  return m && (teams || []).some(t => t.id === Number(m[1])) ? `team:${Number(m[1])}` : "private";
}

// Zeile unter dem Namen einer Team-Karte; gelöschte und ausgetretene Ersteller heißen „ehemaliges Mitglied“
export function creatorText(p) {
  if (p?.team_id == null) return "";
  return p.creator && p.creator_is_member ? `von ${p.creator.name}` : "von ehemaligem Mitglied";
}

// Projektauswahl: Gruppe „Privat“ zuerst, dann je Team nach Name; die Reihenfolge der Projekte bleibt
export function groupProjects(list) {
  const priv = { key: "private", label: "Privat", items: [] }, byTeam = new Map();
  for (const p of list || []) {
    if (p.team_id == null) { priv.items.push(p); continue; }
    if (!byTeam.has(p.team_id)) byTeam.set(p.team_id, { key: `team:${p.team_id}`, label: p.team_name || `Team ${p.team_id}`, items: [] });
    byTeam.get(p.team_id).items.push(p);
  }
  const teams = [...byTeam.values()].sort((a, b) => a.label.localeCompare(b.label, "de") || a.key.localeCompare(b.key));
  return [priv, ...teams].filter(g => g.items.length);
}
```

```js
// Schritt 1: Projektbibliothek – private und Team-Bibliotheken mit Vorschaubild, Start für neue Projekte
import { api, h, clear, icon, dt, toast, fail, dialog, confirmDlg, debounce } from "./lib.js";
import { S, go, onLeave, rememberProject, rememberedProjectId, forgetProject, jobBadge } from "./app.js";
import { ROLE_TEXT, creatorText, projectRights, validScope } from "./rights.js";

const SORTS = { updated: "Zuletzt geändert", name: "Name", created: "Angelegt" };
const plural = (n, one, many) => `${n} ${n === 1 ? one : many}`;
const COLL = new Intl.Collator("de", { sensitivity: "base" });

function loadView() {
  try {
    const v = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("lib.view") || "{}");
    return { sort: Object.hasOwn(SORTS, v.sort) ? v.sort : "updated", archived: v.archived === true };
  } catch { return { sort: "updated", archived: false }; }
}
function saveView(v) { try { localStorage.setItem("lib.view", JSON.stringify(v)); } catch { /* */ } }
// Bereich der Bibliothek (Spec Teams §7): "private" oder "team:<id>", in diesem Browser gemerkt
function loadScope() { try { return localStorage.getItem("lib.scope") || "private"; } catch { return "private"; } }
function saveScope(v) { try { localStorage.setItem("lib.scope", v); } catch { /* */ } }
const teamOf = scope => (scope.startsWith("team:") ? Number(scope.slice(5)) : null);

// Neues Projekt: leerer Name ist erlaubt, der Server vergibt dann Datum und Uhrzeit; teamId = im Team anlegen
export async function newProject(teamId = null) {
  const inp = h("input", { class: "input", placeholder: "z. B. Halteplatte Lagersitz", maxlength: 120, "aria-label": "Name" });
  const r = await dialog({ title: "Neues Projekt",
    body: h("div", { class: "field" }, h("label", {}, "Name"), inp, h("span", { class: "hint" }, "Leer lassen = Name aus Datum und Uhrzeit.")),
    actions: [{ label: "Abbrechen", value: null }, { label: "Anlegen", primary: true, submit: true, run: () => ({ name: inp.value.trim() }) }] });
  if (!r) return;
  const at = location.pathname + location.search;
  try {
    S.project = await api("/api/projects", { method: "POST", body: teamId ? { name: r.name, team_id: teamId } : { name: r.name } });
    rememberProject();
    if (location.pathname + location.search === at) go("/fotos");   // inzwischen weitergeklickt: dort bleiben
  } catch (e) { fail(e); }
}

export default async function (main) {
  const view = loadView();
  let list = [], teams = [], scope = loadScope(), query = "", loadSeq = 0, opening = false;
  let cards = new Map(), lower = new Map();   // je Projekt-ID Karte und kleingeschriebener Name, je load() einmal gebaut
  const search = h("input", { class: "input", type: "search", placeholder: "Projekt suchen …", "aria-label": "Projekte durchsuchen", maxlength: 120 });
  const sortSel = h("select", { class: "input", "aria-label": "Sortierung" },
    Object.entries(SORTS).map(([k, t]) => h("option", { value: k, selected: k === view.sort }, t)));
  const archCb = h("input", { type: "checkbox", checked: view.archived });
  const grid = h("div", { class: "libgrid" });
  const tools = h("div", { class: "libtools" },
    h("div", { class: "libsearch" }, icon("search", 16), search),
    h("label", { class: "chips small" }, h("span", { class: "muted" }, "Sortieren"), sortSel),
    h("label", { class: "check small" }, archCb, "Archivierte zeigen"));
  const scopeBar = h("div", { class: "chips libscope", role: "group", "aria-label": "Bibliothek wählen", hidden: true });
  const newBtn = h("button", { class: "btn primary", onclick: () => newProject(teamOf(scope)) }, icon("plus"), "Neues Projekt");
  main.append(
    h("div", { class: "head" }, h("div", { class: "t" }, h("h1", {}, "Projekte"),
      h("p", { class: "lead" }, "Alle Werkstücke mit Fotos, Maßen und CAD-Modellen – privat oder gemeinsam im Team. Von hier aus beginnt jedes neue Projekt.")),
    newBtn),
    h("div", { class: "chips", style: { justifyContent: "space-between" } }, scopeBar,
      h("a", { class: "small", href: "/teams" }, icon("users", 16), " Teams verwalten")),
    tools, grid);

  search.addEventListener("input", debounce(() => { query = search.value.trim().toLowerCase(); if (main.isConnected) draw(); }, 150));
  sortSel.addEventListener("change", () => { view.sort = sortSel.value; saveView(view); draw(); });
  archCb.addEventListener("change", () => { view.archived = archCb.checked; saveView(view); draw(); });

  // offenes Menü „…“: eines zur Zeit, Klick daneben schließt
  let menuClose = null;
  const outside = () => menuClose?.();
  document.addEventListener("click", outside);
  onLeave(() => document.removeEventListener("click", outside));

  // nur die juengste Antwort uebernehmen; Karten (mit Vorschaubild) nur hier neu bauen, damit Suchen
  // und Sortieren laufende Bildladevorgaenge nicht abbrechen
  async function load() {
    const n = ++loadSeq;
    const t = await api("/api/teams");
    const sc = validScope(scope, t.teams);
    let l;
    try { l = await api(`/api/projects?scope=${encodeURIComponent(sc)}`); }
    catch (e) {   // Team inzwischen verlassen oder gelöscht: still zur privaten Bibliothek (Review Focus 3)
      if (e.status === 404 && sc !== "private" && n === loadSeq) { scope = "private"; saveScope(scope); return load(); }
      throw e;
    }
    if (n !== loadSeq || !main.isConnected) return;
    teams = t.teams;
    if (sc !== scope) { scope = sc; saveScope(sc); }
    menuClose?.();
    list = l;
    drawScopes();
    const cur = rememberedProjectId();
    cards = new Map(list.map(p => [p.id, card(p, p.id === cur)]));
    lower = new Map(list.map(p => [p.id, p.name.toLowerCase()]));
    draw();
  }
  const reload = () => load().catch(fail);

  function drawScopes() {
    const chip = (sc, label) => h("button", { class: "chip", type: "button", "aria-pressed": String(sc === scope),
      onclick: () => { if (sc === scope) return; scope = sc; saveScope(sc); reload(); } }, label);
    clear(scopeBar, chip("private", "Privat"), teams.map(t => chip(`team:${t.id}`, t.name)));
    scopeBar.hidden = !teams.length;   // ohne Team wie bisher nur die private Bibliothek
    const t = teams.find(x => `team:${x.id}` === scope);
    newBtn.disabled = t?.my_role === "lesend";
    newBtn.title = newBtn.disabled ? "Mit der Rolle Lesend lassen sich keine Projekte anlegen" : "";
  }

  async function open(p, path) {
    if (opening) return;   // Doppelklick: nur einmal laden
    opening = true;
    try {
      const proj = await api(`/api/projects/${p.id}`);
      if (!main.isConnected) return;   // inzwischen andere Seite
      S.project = proj;
      rememberProject();
      go(path);
    } catch (e) { fail(e); if (main.isConnected) reload(); }
    finally { opening = false; }
  }

  function sorted() {
    const out = list.filter(p => (view.archived || !p.archived) && (!query || lower.get(p.id).includes(query)));
    const by = { updated: (a, b) => String(b.updated_at).localeCompare(String(a.updated_at)) || b.id - a.id,
      created: (a, b) => String(b.created_at).localeCompare(String(a.created_at)) || b.id - a.id,
      name: (a, b) => COLL.compare(a.name, b.name) || b.id - a.id }[view.sort];
    return out.sort(by);
  }

  function draw() {
    menuClose?.();
    tools.hidden = !list.length;
    if (!list.length) { clear(grid, emptyState()); grid.classList.add("one"); return; }
    grid.classList.remove("one");
    const shown = sorted();
    if (!shown.length) {
      const onlyArchived = !query && !view.archived && list.every(p => p.archived);
      clear(grid, h("div", { class: "empty card" }, icon("folder", 32),
        onlyArchived ? "Alle Projekte sind archiviert. „Archivierte zeigen“ blendet sie ein." : "Kein Projekt passt zur Suche."));
      grid.classList.add("one");
      return;
    }
    grid.replaceChildren(...shown.map(p => cards.get(p.id)));
  }

  function emptyState() {
    const t = teams.find(x => `team:${x.id}` === scope);
    if (t) {
      return h("section", { class: "empty card" }, icon("folder", 32), `Im Team „${t.name}“ gibt es noch keine Projekte.`,
        t.my_role === "lesend" ? null : h("button", { class: "btn sm primary", onclick: () => newProject(t.id) }, icon("plus", 16), "Neues Projekt"));
    }
    const st = (n, title, body) => h("li", {}, h("span", { class: "num" }, n), h("span", {}, h("strong", {}, title), h("br"), body));
    return h("section", { class: "card", "aria-labelledby": "start-h" },
      h("h2", { id: "start-h" }, "So geht es los"),
      h("ol", { class: "nextsteps libstart" },
        st("1", "Messunterlage drucken", h("a", { href: "/anleitung#unterlage", class: "small" }, "PDF erzeugen und richtig drucken")),
        st("2", "Werkstück fotografieren", h("a", { href: "/anleitung#fotografieren", class: "small" }, "Tipps zum Fotografieren")),
        st("3", "Neues Projekt anlegen", h("button", { class: "btn sm primary", style: { marginTop: "6px" }, onclick: () => newProject() }, icon("plus", 16), "Neues Projekt"))));
  }

  function thumb(p) {
    const ph = () => h("div", { class: "ph" }, icon("camera", 30), h("span", { class: "small" }, "Noch keine Fotos"));
    if (!p.thumb?.url) return h("div", { class: "pthumb" }, ph());
    const img = h("img", { src: p.thumb.url, alt: "", loading: "lazy", decoding: "async" });
    const box = h("div", { class: "pthumb" }, img,
      p.thumb.kind === "model" ? h("span", { class: "badge3d", title: "Bild des 3D-Modells" }, icon("cube", 14), "3D") : null);
    img.addEventListener("error", () => clear(box, ph()), { once: true });   // Bild fehlt: Platzhalter
    return box;
  }

  // Einträge im Menü „…“ nach den Rechten am Projekt (Spec Teams §7); nicht erlaubte Einträge fehlen
  function menuItems(p, item) {
    const r = projectRights(p, S.me.id), targets = writableTeams();
    return [
      r.write ? item("Umbenennen …", () => rename(p)) : null,
      r.write ? item(p.archived ? "Wiederherstellen" : "Archivieren", () => archive(p)) : null,
      r.move && p.team_id == null && targets.length ? item("In Team verschieben …", () => moveToTeam(p)) : null,
      r.move && p.team_id != null ? item("In privat verschieben", () => moveToPrivate(p)) : null,
      r.delete ? h("div", { class: "sep" }) : null,
      r.delete ? item("Löschen …", () => remove(p), "danger") : null,
    ];
  }

  function card(p, isCur) {
    const lj = p.last_job;
    const menuBtn = h("button", { class: "iconbtn", type: "button", "aria-label": `Weitere Aktionen für ${p.name}`, "aria-haspopup": "menu", "aria-expanded": "false" }, icon("dots"));
    const menu = h("div", { class: "menu", role: "menu", hidden: true });
    const item = (label, fn, cls = "") => h("button", { role: "menuitem", class: cls, type: "button", tabindex: "-1",
      onclick: async ev => { ev.stopPropagation(); closeMenu(false); try { await fn(); } catch (e) { fail(e); } } }, label);
    const entries = menuItems(p, item);
    clear(menu, entries);
    const items = () => [...menu.querySelectorAll("[role=menuitem]")];
    function closeMenu(focusBtn = true) {
      if (menu.hidden) return;
      menu.hidden = true; menuBtn.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
      if (menuClose === close) menuClose = null;
      if (focusBtn) menuBtn.focus();
    }
    const close = () => closeMenu(false);
    function openMenu(focusIdx = 0) {
      menuClose?.();
      menu.hidden = false; menuBtn.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true");
      menuClose = close;
      items()[focusIdx]?.focus();
    }
    menuBtn.addEventListener("click", ev => { ev.stopPropagation(); if (menu.hidden) openMenu(); else closeMenu(); });
    menuBtn.addEventListener("keydown", ev => {
      if (ev.key === "ArrowDown") { ev.preventDefault(); openMenu(0); }
      else if (ev.key === "ArrowUp") { ev.preventDefault(); openMenu(items().length - 1); }
    });
    menu.addEventListener("click", ev => ev.stopPropagation());
    menu.addEventListener("keydown", ev => {
      const it = items(), i = it.indexOf(document.activeElement);
      if (ev.key === "Escape") { ev.preventDefault(); ev.stopPropagation(); closeMenu(); }
      else if (ev.key === "ArrowDown") { ev.preventDefault(); it[(i + 1) % it.length].focus(); }
      else if (ev.key === "ArrowUp") { ev.preventDefault(); it[(i - 1 + it.length) % it.length].focus(); }
      else if (ev.key === "Home") { ev.preventDefault(); it[0].focus(); }
      else if (ev.key === "End") { ev.preventDefault(); it[it.length - 1].focus(); }
      else if (ev.key === "Tab") closeMenu(false);
    });

    const badges = [isCur ? h("span", { class: "badge info" }, "Zuletzt geöffnet") : null,
      p.archived ? h("span", { class: "badge off" }, "archiviert") : null,
      p.my_role === "lesend" ? h("span", { class: "badge off" }, ROLE_TEXT.lesend) : null].filter(Boolean);
    const stop = fn => ev => { ev.stopPropagation(); fn(); };
    const el = h("article", { class: `pcard${p.archived ? " archived" : ""}${isCur ? " current" : ""}`, "aria-label": p.name,
      onclick: ev => { if (!ev.target.closest("button,a,.menu")) open(p, "/fotos"); } },
      thumb(p),
      h("div", { class: "pbody" },
        badges.length ? h("div", { class: "chips" }, badges) : null,
        h("h2", { class: "pname", title: p.name }, p.name),
        p.team_id != null ? h("span", { class: "small muted pcreator" }, creatorText(p)) : null,
        h("span", { class: "small muted" }, `${plural(p.n_photos, "Foto", "Fotos")} · ${plural(p.n_measurements, "Maß", "Maße")}`),
        h("span", { class: "small muted" }, `Zuletzt geändert ${dt(p.updated_at)}`),
        lj ? h("span", { class: "pjob small" }, jobBadge(lj.status),
          h("span", { class: "pjobname" }, `Auftrag ${lj.name}${lj.current_version ? ` · v${lj.current_version}` : ""}`)) : null),
      h("div", { class: "pacts" },
        h("button", { class: "btn sm primary", type: "button", onclick: stop(() => open(p, "/fotos")) }, "Öffnen"),
        h("button", { class: "btn sm", type: "button", onclick: stop(() => open(p, "/messen")) }, "Messen"),
        lj ? h("button", { class: "btn sm", type: "button", onclick: stop(() => open(p, `/cad/${lj.id}`)) }, icon("cube", 14), "Modell") : null,
        entries.some(Boolean) ? h("div", { class: "rel", style: { marginLeft: "auto" } }, menuBtn, menu) : null));
    return el;
  }

  const writableTeams = () => teams.filter(t => t.my_role === "leiter" || t.my_role === "mitglied");
  function pickTeam(title, label) {
    const sel = h("select", { class: "input", "aria-label": "Team" }, writableTeams().map(t => h("option", { value: t.id }, t.name)));
    return dialog({ title, body: h("div", { class: "field" }, h("label", {}, "Team"), sel,
      h("span", { class: "hint" }, "Nur Teams, in denen du Leiter oder Mitglied bist.")),
    actions: [{ label: "Abbrechen", value: null }, { label, primary: true, submit: true, run: () => Number(sel.value) || false }] });
  }

  async function rename(p) {
    const inp = h("input", { class: "input", value: p.name, maxlength: 120, "aria-label": "Name" });
    const name = await dialog({ title: "Projekt umbenennen", body: h("div", { class: "field" }, h("label", {}, "Name"), inp),
      actions: [{ label: "Abbrechen", value: null }, { label: "Speichern", primary: true, submit: true, run: () => inp.value.trim() || false }] });
    if (!name || name === p.name) return;
    const r = await api(`/api/projects/${p.id}`, { method: "PATCH", body: { name } });
    if (S.project?.id === p.id) S.project = r;
    toast("Projekt umbenannt", "ok");
    await reload();
  }
  async function archive(p) {
    const r = await api(`/api/projects/${p.id}`, { method: "PATCH", body: { archived: !p.archived } });
    if (S.project?.id === p.id) S.project = r;
    toast(p.archived ? "Projekt wiederhergestellt" : "Projekt archiviert", "ok");
    await reload();
  }
  async function moveToTeam(p) {
    const tid = await pickTeam(`„${p.name}“ in ein Team verschieben`, "Verschieben");
    if (!tid) return;
    const r = await api(`/api/projects/${p.id}/move`, { method: "POST", body: { team_id: tid } });
    if (S.project?.id === p.id) S.project = r;
    toast(`In „${r.team_name}“ verschoben – die CAD-Aufträge sind mitgekommen`, "ok");
    await reload();
  }
  async function moveToPrivate(p) {
    if (!await confirmDlg(`„${p.name}“ in privat verschieben?`,
      "Das Projekt verlässt das Team und gehört danach dir; die CAD-Aufträge kommen mit. Die anderen Mitglieder sehen es nicht mehr.",
      "In privat verschieben")) return;
    const r = await api(`/api/projects/${p.id}/move`, { method: "POST", body: { team_id: null } });
    if (S.project?.id === p.id) S.project = r;
    toast("In die private Bibliothek verschoben", "ok");
    await reload();
  }
  async function remove(p) {
    const who = p.team_id != null ? `für alle Mitglieder von „${p.team_name}“ ` : "";
    if (!await confirmDlg(`„${p.name}“ löschen?`,
      `Fotos, Messungen, Teile und die am Modell gemerkten Messschieberwerte dieses Projekts werden ${who}endgültig gelöscht. `
      + "Die CAD-Aufträge bleiben unter „Meine Aufträge“ ihrer Ersteller erhalten, aber ohne Fotos für den Foto-Abgleich.", "Endgültig löschen", true)) return;
    await api(`/api/projects/${p.id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
    if (rememberedProjectId() === p.id) forgetProject();
    if (S.project?.id === p.id) S.project = null;
    toast("Projekt gelöscht", "ok");
    await reload();
  }

  await load();
}
```

Nach der Importzeile von `./lib.js` (Z. 2–3) einfügen:

```js
import { groupProjects } from "./rights.js";
```

`projectPicker` vollständig ersetzen:

```js
export function projectPicker(onChange) {
  const sel = h("select", { class: "input", "aria-label": "Projekt", style: { minWidth: "220px", maxWidth: "320px" } });
  // gruppiert nach „Privat“ und je Team (Spec Teams §7), Projekte aus scope=all
  const fill = list => {
    const opt = p => h("option", { value: p.id, selected: p.id === S.project.id },
      `${p.name}${p.archived ? " (archiviert)" : ""} · ${p.n_photos} Fotos · ${p.n_measurements} Maße`);
    clear(sel, groupProjects(list).map(g => h("optgroup", { label: g.label }, g.items.map(opt))),
      h("option", { value: "new" }, "+ Neues Projekt …"), h("option", { value: "rename" }, "Projekt umbenennen …"));
  };
  const load = () => api("/api/projects?scope=all");
  load().then(fill).catch(fail);
  // data-ro="ok": bleibt in der Rolle Lesend bedienbar (Projekt wechseln)
  const wrap = h("label", { class: "field", style: { gap: "4px" }, "data-ro": "ok" }, h("span", { class: "label" }, "Projekt"), sel);
  wrap.refresh = () => load().then(fill).catch(() => {});
  sel.addEventListener("change", async () => {
    const v = sel.value;
    try {
      if (v === "new" || v === "rename") {
        const inp = h("input", { class: "input", value: v === "rename" ? S.project.name : "", placeholder: "z. B. Halteplatte Lagersitz", maxlength: 120 });
        const name = await dialog({ title: v === "new" ? "Neues Projekt" : "Projekt umbenennen",
          body: h("div", { class: "field" }, h("label", {}, "Name"), inp),
          actions: [{ label: "Abbrechen", value: null }, { label: "Speichern", primary: true, submit: true, run: () => inp.value.trim() || false }] });
        if (name) {
          if (v === "new") S.project = await api("/api/projects", { method: "POST", body: { name } });
          else S.project = await api(`/api/projects/${S.project.id}`, { method: "PATCH", body: { name } });
          rememberProject();
        }
      } else {
        S.project = await api(`/api/projects/${v}`);
        rememberProject();
      }
      onChange();
    } catch (e) { fail(e); }
    fill(await load());
  });
  return wrap;
}
```

### Task 8: Oberfläche – Rolle Lesend auf Fotos, Messen und CAD (`app.js`, `photos.js`, `measure.js`, `cad.js`)

**Files:**
- Modify: `web/static/js/rights.js` (zwei Exporte am Dateiende), `tests/js/rights.test.mjs` (Importzeile, ein Test)
- Modify: `web/static/js/app.js` (Importzeile aus Task 7; `api.onNotFound` nach `api.onMustChange` Z. 66; neue Exporte direkt nach `forgetProject`)
- Modify: `web/static/js/lib.js:26` (`api`: Meldung bei 404)
- Modify: `web/static/js/photos.js`, `web/static/js/measure.js`, `web/static/js/cad.js` (Zeilen laut Tabellen in Step 4)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `my_role`, `team_name` am Projekt (Task 5) und am Auftrag (`GET /api/jobs/{jid}`, Task 5); `projectPicker` mit `data-ro="ok"` (Task 7).
- Produces:
  - `rights.isReadOnly(p) -> boolean` (`my_role === "lesend"`), `rights.readOnlyText(p) -> string` („Nur ansehen – Rolle Lesend im Team <Name>“).
  - `app.readOnlyNote(p) -> HTMLElement` (`.note.info.ro-note`, `role="status"`).
  - `lib.api.onNotFound: ((meldung: string) => void) | null` – bei jeder 404 vor dem Werfen aufgerufen; `app.js` setzt ihn (Spec §8: Zugriff auf das offene Projekt verloren → Bibliothek).
  - `app.readOnlyMode(root, slot) -> () => void` – sperrt in `root` alle `button, input, select, textarea` außer innerhalb von `[data-ro="ok"]`, solange `S.project` die Rolle Lesend hat, zeigt den Hinweis in `slot`, sperrt nachgezeichnete Elemente per `MutationObserver` (einmal je Frame); die zurückgegebene Funktion nach einem Projektwechsel aufrufen; gibt nur selbst gesperrte Elemente wieder frei (`data-ro-locked`).

```js
test("api meldet 404 an onNotFound (Spec §8: Zugriff verloren -> Bibliothek)", async () => {
  const seen = [], old = globalThis.fetch;
  globalThis.fetch = async () => new Response(JSON.stringify({ detail: "Projekt nicht gefunden" }),
    { status: 404, headers: { "content-type": "application/json" } });
  api.onNotFound = m => seen.push(m);
  try { await assert.rejects(api("/api/projects/7"), e => e.status === 404 && e.message === "Projekt nicht gefunden"); }
  finally { globalThis.fetch = old; api.onNotFound = null; }
  assert.deepEqual(seen, ["Projekt nicht gefunden"]);
});

test("isReadOnly und readOnlyText: nur die Rolle Lesend", () => {
  assert.equal(isReadOnly({ my_role: "lesend" }), true);
  for (const r of ["besitzer", "admin", "leiter", "mitglied", null]) assert.equal(isReadOnly({ my_role: r }), false, String(r));
  assert.equal(isReadOnly(null), false);
  assert.equal(readOnlyText({ team_name: "Werkstatt" }), "Nur ansehen – Rolle Lesend im Team Werkstatt");
  assert.equal(readOnlyText({}), "Nur ansehen – Rolle Lesend im Team");
});
```
